- **syncdb**: Sincroniza os índices SQLite (FTS5) do pack .cursor/, do workspace .workspace/ e dos docs técnicos locais em E:\.docs\ com o estado actual dos ficheiros. ([cslsolucoes/SkillsORM](https://github.com/cslsolucoes/SkillsORM/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/syncdb.md) · ★5 · 3 copies)

This command lets the user swap in their own template: store the template files, capture usage instructions (source e ([shashank-1010/Vanta-Search](https://github.com/shashank-1010/Vanta-Search/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/syntex-add-template.md))

- **syntex-interview**: You are preparing the user for a real, scheduled interview on one of their applications. This command ([shashank-1010/Vanta-Search](https://github.com/shashank-1010/Vanta-Search/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/syntex-interview.md))
- **syntex-notion-sync**: You are publishing a **read-only view** of the job search into the user's Notion workspace: one database row per job, with a detailed page per shortlisted match. The repo files stay the system of record - `job_scraper/seen_jobs.json` owns scraped/ranked jobs and `job_search_tracker.csv` owns applications. Notion is a disposable presentation layer on top of them; nothing ever syncs back. ([shashank-1010/Vanta-Search](https://github.com/shashank-1010/Vanta-Search/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/syntex-notion-sync.md))
- **syntex-outcome**: You are recording what happened to a job application: progress updates (interview invitations, stages completed, offers) and final resolutions (hired, rejected, no response). - **syntex-rank**: You are batch-scoring the jobs that `/syntex-scrape` has collected, so the user can decide where to spend `/syntex-apply` effort. `/syntex-scrape` finds and dedupes postings; `/syntex-apply` evaluates one at a time in depth. `/syntex-rank` is the bridge: it scores every new posting against the fit framework and returns a ranked shortlist. - **syntex-setup**: You are running the onboarding setup for the Syntex framework. - **synxis-pms-property**: Check SynXis PMS property connectivity, credential/mock-mode state, and the available PMS tool surface. ([lesleslie/synxis-pms-mcp](https://github.com/lesleslie/synxis-pms-mcp/blob/HEAD/commands/synxis-pms-property.md) · ★2)
- **synxis-pms-room**: Inspect SynXis PMS room status, type, floor, features, and occupancy for a given room ID. ([lesleslie/synxis-pms-mcp](https://github.com/lesleslie/synxis-pms-mcp/blob/HEAD/commands/synxis-pms-room.md) · ★2)
- **synxis-pms-stay**: Drive the SynXis PMS guest stay lifecycle — check in, check out, or review the folio for a reservation. ([lesleslie/synxis-pms-mcp](https://github.com/lesleslie/synxis-pms-mcp/blob/HEAD/commands/synxis-pms-stay.md) · ★2)
- **sys**: &gt; Inspect your machine — devices, EPs, and runtime versions at a glance. ([microsoft/winml-cli](https://github.com/microsoft/winml-cli/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/sys.md) · ★51)
- **sys**: View information about the system. ([astrohackerlabs/nutorch](https://github.com/astrohackerlabs/nutorch/blob/HEAD/website/content/docs/shell/commands/sys.md) · ★12)
- **sys**: Execute a machine-code routine at 6502 address *addr*. Optional byte arguments load the 6502 `A`, `X`, and `Y` registers before the call. The routine must return with `RTS` to resume BASIC. ([barryw/NovaVM](https://github.com/barryw/NovaVM/blob/HEAD/docs/help/reference/commands/sys.md) · ★2)
- **sys**: Answer a question about this machine with live read-only probes (disk, memory, load, processes) ([bamr87/bashos](https://github.com/bamr87/bashos/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sys.md))
- **sys-debug**: 4-phase systematic root cause investigation. No guessing. Hypothesize ranked causes → Instrument logging → Observe output → Fix confirmed root cause only. Generates temporary instrumentation, analyzes evidence, applies targeted fix. ([usmanmughaltaleemabad/One-Shot-Plugin](https://github.com/usmanmughaltaleemabad/One-Shot-Plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/sys-debug.md))
